<details><summary>AI, NOT PATRICK (context)</summary>

◆ Got it. The question is the chain of events, not the source. Here it is for fire, step by step.

**What fire actually is:** molecules moving very fast. Heat is just particles jostling, and when they jostle hard enough, fuel and oxygen tear apart and recombine. That reaction releases light and more heat, and that's flame. With no fuel, push the air hard enough and it rips into plasma, which is glowing, burning air.

**So to make fire, magic has to make particles at one spot move violently fast.** The real question is what grabs those particles and shakes them.

**Candidate: the soul plucks th…
</details>

It gives the system two skills instead of one:
